- **memory-guard**: The agent pool ([Pool & Sessions](./pool)) caps how many agent **processes** run at once. It has no idea how much RAM any one of them uses — an idle agent at ~150 MB and one driving a browser at ~2 GB count as the same slot. A single runaway agent (a browser leak, a bad loop) can take the whole machine down with it, wick included. - **memory-model**: What the agent layer remembers, what is cached, what is per-turn vs per-entity. This is *not* a vector store; Sentinel does not embed-and-retrieve over arbitrary corpora.
